128→// ensureTemplatingExists ensures templating.list exists\n   129→func ensureTemplatingExists(dashboard map[string]interface{}) {\n   130→\tif templating, ok := ensurePanelsHaveUniqueIds(dashboard map[string]interface{}) {\n   156→\tpanels := getPanels(dashboard)\n   157→\tif len(panels) == 0 {\n   158→\t\treturn\n   159→\t}\n   160→\n   161→\tids := make(map[float64]bool)\n   162→\tnextPanelId := getNextPanelId(panels)\n   163→\n   164→\tfor _, panel := range panels {\n   165→\t\tif panelID, ok := panel[\"id\"].(float64); ok && panelID > 0 {\n   166→\t\t\tif ids[panelID] {\n   167→\t\t\t\t// Duplicate ID found, assign new one\n   168→\t\t\t\tpanel[\"id\"] = float64(nextPanelId)\n   169→\t\t\t\tnextPanelId++\n   170→\t\t\t} else {\n   171→\t\t\t\t// Valid unique ID, keep it\n   172→\t\t\t\tids[panelID] = true\n   173→\t\t\t}\n   174→\t\t} else {\n   175→\t\t\t// No ID or invalid ID, assign new one\n   176→\t\t\tpanel[\"id\"] = float64(nextPanelId)\n   177→\t\t\tnextPanelId++\n   178→\t\t}\n   179→\t}\n   180→}\n   181→\n   182→// getNextPanelId finds the next available panel ID\n   183→func getNextPanelId(panels []map[string]interface{}) int {\n   184→\tmax := 0\n   185→\tfor _, panel := range panels {\n   186→\t\tif panelID, ok := panel[\"id\"].(float64); ok && panelID > 0 {\n   187→\t\t\tif int(panelID) > max {\n   188→\t\t\t\tmax = int(panelID)\n   189→\t\t\t}\n   190→\t\t}\n   191→\t}\n   192→\treturn max + 1\n   193→}\n   194→\n   195→// sortPanelsByGridPos sorts panels by grid position (y first, then x)\n   196→func sortPanelsByGridPos(dashboard map[string]interface{}) {\n   197→\tpanels := getPanels(dashboard)\n   198→\tif len(panels) == 0 {\n   199→\t\treturn\n   200→\t}\n   201→\n   202→\tsort.SliceStable(panels, func(i, j int) bool {\n   203→\t\tpanelA := panels[i]\n   204→\t\tpanelB := panels[j]\n   205→\n   206→\t\tgridPosA, okA := panelA[\"gridPos\"].(map[string]interface{})\n   207→\t\tgridPosB, okB := panelB[\"gridPos\"].(map[string]interface{})\n   208→\n   209→\t\tif !okA || !okB {\n   210→\t\t\treturn false\n   211→\t\t}\n   212→\n   213→\t\tyA, okA := gridPosA[\"y\"].(float64)\n   214→\t\tyB, okB := gridPosB[\"y\"].(float64)\n   215→\n   216→\t\tif !okA || !okB {\n   217→\t\t\treturn false\n   218→\t\t}\n   219→\n   220→\t\tif yA == yB {\n   221→\t\t\t// Same row, sort by x\n   222→\t\t\txA, okA := gridPosA[\"x\"].(float64)\n   223→\t\t\txB, okB := gridPosB[\"x\"].(float64)\n   224→\n   225→\t\t\tif !okA || !okB {\n   226→\t\t\t\treturn false\n   227→\t\t\t}\n   228→\n   229→\t\t\treturn xA < xB\n   230→\t\t}\n   231→\n   232→\t\treturn yA < yB\n   233→\t})\n   234→}\n   235→\n   236→// addBuiltInAnnotationQuery adds the built-in \"Annotations & Alerts\" annotation\n   237→func addBuiltInAnnotationQuery(dashboard map[string]interface{}) {\n   238→\tannotations, ok := 272→\tannotations[\"list\"] = append([]interface{}{builtInAnnotation}, list...)\n   273→}\n   274→\n   275→// initMeta initializes meta properties with defaults\n   276→func initMeta(dashboard 317→\t}\n   318→}\n   319→\n   320→// removeNullValuesFromVariables removes null values from variable.current.value\n   321→func removeNullValuesFromVariables(dashboard map[string]interface{}) {\n   322→\ttemplating, ok := dashboard[\"templating\"].(map[string]interface{})\n   323→\tif !ok {\n   324→\t\treturn\n   325→\t}\n   326→\n   327→\tlist, ok := templating[\"list\"].([]interface{})\n   328→\tif !ok || len(list) == 0 {\n   329→\t\treturn\n   330→\t}\n   331→\n   332→\tfor _, item := range list {\n   333→\t\tif variable, ok := item.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n   334→\t\t\tif arrays\n   341→\t\t\t\t\t\thasNull := false\n   342→\t\t\t\t\t\tfor _, v := range valueArray {\n   343→\t\t\t\t\t\t\tif v == nil {\n   344→\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\thasNull = true\n 352→\t\t\t\t}\n   353→\t\t\t}\n   354→\t\t}\n   355→\t}\n   356→}\n   357→\n   358→// ensureQueryIds ensures all queries have refId\n   359→func ensureQueryIds(panel map[string]interface{}) {\n   360→\ttargets, ok := panel[\"targets\"].([]interface{})\n   361→\tif !ok || len(targets) == 0 {\n   362→\t\treturn\n   363→\t}\n   364→\n   365→\t// Check if any target is missing refId\n   366→\thasMissingRefId := false\n   367→\tfor _, target := range targets {\n   368→\t\tif targetMap, ok := target.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n   369→\t\t\tif targetMap[\"refId\"] == nil {\n   370→\t\t\t\thasMissingRefId = true\n   371→\t\t\t\tbreak\n   372→\t\t\t}\n   373→\t\t}\n   374→\t}\n   375→\n   376→\tif hasMissingRefId {\n   377→\t\t// Find existing refIds\n   378→\t\texistingRefIds := make(map[string]bool)\n   379→\t\tfor _, target := range targets {\n   380→\t\t\tif targetMap, ok := target.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n   381→\t\t\t\tif refId, ok := targetMap[\"refId\"].(string); ok {\n   382→\t\t\t\t\texistingRefIds[refId] = true\n   383→\t\t\t\t}\n   384→\t\t\t}\n   385→\t\t}\n   386→\n   387→\t\t// Assign refIds to targets that don't have them\n   388→\t\tletters := \"ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ\"\n   389→\t\tletterIndex := 0\n   390→\n   391→\t\tfor _, target := range targets {\n   392→\t\t\tif targetMap, ok := target.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n   393→\t\t\t\tif targetMap[\"refId\"] == nil {\n   394→\t\t\t\t\t// Find next available refId\n   395→\t\t\t\t\tfor letterIndex < len(letters) {\n   396→\t\t\t\t\t\trefId := string(letters[letterIndex])\n   397→\t\t\t\t\t\tif !existingRefIds[refId] {\n   398→\t\t\t\t\t\t\ttargetMap[\"refId\"] = refId\n   399→\t\t\t\t\t\t\texistingRefIds[refId] = true\n   400→\t\t\t\t\t\t\tbreak\n   401→\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n   402→\t\t\t\t\t\tletterIndex++\n   403→\t\t\t\t\t}\n   404→\t\t\t\t\tletterIndex++\n   405→\t\t\t\t}\n   406→\t\t\t}\n   407→\t\t}\n   408→\t}\n   409→}\n   410→\n   411→// getPanels extracts all panels from the dashboard (including nested panels)\n   412→// This matches the frontend's depth-first iteration order in panelIterator()\n   413→func getPanels(dashboard map[string]interface{}) []map[string]interface{} {\n   414→\tvar panels []map[string]interface{}\n   415→\n   416→\t// Get top-level panels\n   417→\tif dashboardPanels, 420→\t\t\t\t// Add top-level panel first\n   421→\t\t\t\tpanels = append(panels, panel)\n   422→\n   423→\t\t\t\t// Then add its nested panels (depth-first order)\n   424→\t\t\t\tif nestedPanels, ok := panel[\"panels\"].([]interface{}); ok {\n   425→\t\t\t\t\tfor _, nestedPanelInterface := range nestedPanels {\n   426→\t\t\t\t\t\tif nestedPanel, ok := nestedPanelInterface.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n   427→\t\t\t\t\t\t\tpanels = append(panels, nestedPanel)\n   428→\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n   429→\t\t\t\t\t}\n   430→\t\t\t\t}\n   431→\t\t\t}\n   432→\t\t}\n   433→\t}\n   434→\n   435→\treturn panels\n   436→}\n   437→\n   438→// cleanupPanelForSaveWithContext mimics the PanelModel.getSaveModel() behavior\n   439→// This removes properties that shouldn't be persisted and filters out default values\n   440→func cleanupPanelForSaveWithContext(panel map[string]interface{}, isNested bool) {\n   441→\t// Apply auto-migration logic (matches frontend PanelModel constructor)\n   442→\t// This happens during cleanup phase to match when frontend applies auto-migration\n   443→\t// Only apply auto-migration to top-level panels, not nested ones (matches frontend behavior)\n   444→\tif !isNested {\n   445→\t\tapplyPanelAutoMigration(panel)\n   446→\t}\n   447→\n   448→\t// Library panel specific cleanup (matches frontend behavior)\n   449→\t// Frontend only preserves id, title, gridPos, and libraryPanel for library panels\n   450→\tif libraryPanel, hasLibraryPanel := panel[\"libraryPanel\"]; hasLibraryPanel && libraryPanel != nil {\n   451→\t\t// Create a new panel with only the essential properties\n   452→\t\tessentialProps := map[string]interface{}{\n   453→\t\t\t\"id\":           panel[\"id\"],\n   454→\t\t\t\"title\":        panel[\"title\"],\n   455→\t\t\t\"gridPos\":      panel[\"gridPos\"],\n   456→\t\t\t\"libraryPanel\": libraryPanel,\n   457→\t\t}\n   458→\n   459→\t\t// Clear the original panel and copy back only essential properties\n   460→\t\tfor key := range panel {\n   461→\t\t\tdelete(panel, key)\n   462→\t\t}\n   463→\t\tfor key, value := range essentialProps {\n   464→\t\t\tif value != nil {\n   465→\t\t\t\tpanel[key] = value\n   466→\t\t\t}\n   467→\t\t}\n   468→\t\treturn // Skip the rest of the cleanup for library panels\n   469→\t}\n   470→\n   471→\t// Row panel specific cleanup (matches frontend behavior)\n   472→\tcleanupRowPanelProperties(panel)\n   473→\n   474→\t// Track which properties were present in the input to preserve them even if they become empty\n   475→\toriginalProperties := make(map[string]bool)\n   476→\tfor key := range panel {\n   477→\t\toriginalProperties[key] = true\n   478→\t}\n   479→\t// Properties that should never be persisted (notPersistedProperties)\n   480→\tnotPersistedProps := map[string]bool{\n   481→\t\t\"events\":                  true,\n   482→\t\t\"isViewing\":               true,\n   483→\t\t\"isEditing\":               true,\n   484→\t\t\"isInView\":                true,\n   485→\t\t\"hasRefreshed\":            true,\n   486→\t\t\"cachedPluginOptions\":     true, // This is the key one causing issues\n   487→\t\t\"plugin\":                  true,\n   488→\t\t\"queryRunner\":             true,\n   489→\t\t\"replaceVariables\":        true,\n   490→\t\t\"configRev\":               true,\n   491→\t\t\"hasSavedPanelEditChange\": true,\n   492→\t\t\"getDisplayTitle\":         true,\n   493→\t\t\"dataSupport\":             true,\n   494→\t\t\"key\":                     true,\n   495→\t\t\"isNew\":                   true,\n   496→\t\t\"refreshWhenInView\":       true,\n   497→\t\t\"scopedVars\":              true, // Frontend removes scopedVars from save model\n   498→\t}\n   499→\n   500→\t// Default values that should be filtered out if they match (defaults)\n   501→\tdefaults := map[string]interface{}{\n   502→\t\t\"gridPos\": map[string]interface{}{\n   503→\t\t\t\"x\": float64(0), \"y\": float64(0), \"h\": float64(3), \"w\": float64(6),\n   504→\t\t},\n   505→\t\t\"targets\": []interface{}{\n   506→\t\t\tmap[string]interface{}{\"refId\": \"A\"},\n   507→\t\t},\n   508→\t\t\"cachedPluginOptions\": map[string]interface{}{},\n   509→\t\t\"transparent\":         false,\n   510→\t\t\"options\":             map[string]interface{}{},\n   511→\t\t\"links\":               []interface{}{},\n   512→\t\t\"fieldConfig\": map[string]interface{}{\n   513→\t\t\t\"defaults\":  map[string]interface{}{},\n   514→\t\t\t\"overrides\": []interface{}{},\n   515→\t\t},\n   516→\t\t\"title\": \"\",\n   517→\t}\n   518→\n   519→\t// Remove notPersistedProperties\n   520→\tfor prop := range notPersistedProps {\n   521→\t\tdelete(panel, prop)\n   522→\t}\n   523→\n   524→\t// Filter out properties that match defaults\n   525→\tfor prop, defaultValue := range defaults {\n   526→\t\tif panelValue, exists := panel[prop]; exists {\n   527→\t\t\tif isEqual(panelValue, defaultValue) {\n   528→\t\t\t\tdelete(panel, prop)\n   529→\t\t\t}\n   530→\t\t}\n   531→\t}\n   532→\n   533→\t// Remove empty transformations array unless nested panel had them originally\n   534→\tif transformations, ok := panel[\"transformations\"].([]interface{}); ok && len(transformations) == 0 {\n   535→\t\tif panel[\"_originallyHadTransformations\"] != true || !isNested {\n   536→\t\t\tdelete(panel, \"transformations\")\n   537→\t\t}\n   538→\t}\n   539→\n   540→\t// Remove null values recursively to match frontend's JSON.stringify/parse behavior\n   541→\t// Pass panel type information to help with threshold handling\n   542→\tpanelType := \"\"\n   543→\tif t, ok := panel[\"type\"].(string); ok {\n   544→\t\tpanelType = t\n   545→\t}\n   546→\tremoveNullValuesRecursivelyWithContext(panel, panelType)\n   547→\n   548→\t// Filter out properties that match defaults (matches frontend's isEqual logic)\n   549→\tfilterDefaultValues(panel, originalProperties)\n   550→\n   551→\t// Clean up internal markers\n   552→\tdelete(panel, \"_originallyHadTransformations\")\n   553→\tdelete(panel, \"_originallyHadFieldConfigCustom\")\n   554→}\n   555→\n   556→// filterDefaultValues removes properties that match the default values (matches frontend's isEqual logic)\n   557→func filterDefaultValues(panel map[string]interface{}, originalProperties map[string]bool) {\n   558→\t// Get panel type for panel-specific defaults\n   559→\tpanelType := \"\"\n   560→\tif t, ok := panel[\"type\"].(string); ok {\n   561→\t\tpanelType = t\n   562→\t}\n   563→\n   564→\t// PanelModel defaults from frontend\n   565→\tdefaults := map[string]interface{}{\n   566→\t\t\"gridPos\": map[string]interface{}{\n   567→\t\t\t\"x\": 0, \"y\": 0, \"h\": 3, \"w\": 6,\n   568→\t\t},\n   569→\t\t\"targets\": []interface{}{\n   570→\t\t\tmap[string]interface{}{\"refId\": \"A\"},\n   571→\t\t},\n   572→\t\t\"cachedPluginOptions\": map[string]interface{}{},\n   573→\t\t\"transparent\":         false,\n   574→\t\t\"options\":             map[string]interface{}{},\n   575→\t\t\"links\":               []interface{}{},\n   576→\t\t\"fieldConfig\": map[string]interface{}{\n   577→\t\t\t\"defaults\":  map[string]interface{}{},\n   578→\t\t\t\"overrides\": []interface{}{},\n   579→\t\t},\n   580→\t\t\"title\": \"\",\n   581→\t}\n   582→\n   583→\t// Add panel-specific defaults\n   584→\tif panelType == \"table\" {\n   585→\t\t// Remove legacy table properties (matches frontend getSaveModel filtering)\n   586→\t\t// Exception: preserve original properties for old table panels with autoMigrateFrom=\"table-old\"\n   587→\t\tlegacyTableProps := []string{\"pageSize\", \"scroll\", \"fontSize\", \"showHeader\", \"sort\"}\n   588→\t\tfor _, prop := range legacyTableProps {\n   589→\t\t\tif _, exists := panel[prop]; exists {\n   590→\t\t\t\tif autoMigrateFrom, hasAutoMigrate := panel[\"autoMigrateFrom\"]; hasAutoMigrate && autoMigrateFrom == \"table-old\" {\n   591→\t\t\t\t\tif !originalProperties[prop] {\n   592→\t\t\t\t\t\tdelete(panel, prop)\n   593→\t\t\t\t\t}\n   594→\t\t\t\t} else {\n   595→\t\t\t\t\tdelete(panel, prop)\n   596→\t\t\t\t}\n   597→\t\t\t}\n   598→\t\t}\n   599→\t}\n   600→\n   601→\t// Remove properties that match defaults, but preserve properties that were originally present\n   602→\tfor prop, defaultValue := range defaults {\n   603→\t\tif panelValue, exists := panel[prop]; exists {\n   604→\t\t\tif isEqual(panelValue, defaultValue) {\n   605→\t\t\t\t// Special case: fieldConfig - handle preservation of original custom object first\n   606→\t\t\t\tif prop == \"fieldConfig\" {\n   607→\t\t\t\t\t// Check if we need to preserve the custom object before removing fieldConfig\n   608→\t\t\t\t\tif panel[\"_originallyHadFieldConfigCustom\"] == true {\n   609→\t\t\t\t\t\t// Ensure fieldConfig structure exists with custom object\n   610→\t\t\t\t\t\tif fieldConfig, ok := panelValue.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n   611→\t\t\t\t\t\t\tif defaults, ok := fieldConfig[\"defaults\"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n   612→\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\tif _, hasCustom := defaults[\"custom\"]; !hasCustom {\n   613→\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\tdefaults[\"custom\"] = map[string]interface{}{}\n   614→\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n   615→\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t// Don't remove fieldConfig if we added custom back\n   616→\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n   617→\t\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n   618→\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n   619→\t\t\t\t\t}\n   620→\t\t\t\t\tdelete(panel, prop)\n   621→\t\t\t\t} else {\n   622→\t\t\t\t\t// Only remove if it wasn't originally present in the input\n   623→\t\t\t\t\tif !originalProperties[prop] {\n   624→\t\t\t\t\t\tdelete(panel, prop)\n   625→\t\t\t\t\t}\n   626→\t\t\t\t}\n   627→\t\t\t}\n   628→\t\t}\n   629→\t}\n   630→\n   631→\t// Remove empty targets arrays (frontend removes them in cleanup)\n   632→\tremoveIfDefaultValue(panel, \"targets\", []interface{}{})\n   633→\n   634→\t// Handle case where fieldConfig was removed but originally had custom object\n   635→\tif panel[\"_originallyHadFieldConfigCustom\"] == true {\n   636→\t\tif _, hasFieldConfig := panel[\"fieldConfig\"]; !hasFieldConfig {\n   637→\t\t\t// Recreate fieldConfig with custom object\n   638→\t\t\tpanel[\"fieldConfig\"] = map[string]interface{}{\n   639→\t\t\t\t\"defaults\": map[string]interface{}{\n   640→\t\t\t\t\t\"custom\": map[string]interface{}{},\n   641→\t\t\t\t},\n   642→\t\t\t\t\"overrides\": []interface{}{},\n   643→\t\t\t}\n   644→\t\t}\n   645→\t}\n   646→\n   647→\t// Clean up fieldConfig to match frontend behavior\n   648→\tif fieldConfig, exists := panel[\"fieldConfig\"].(map[string]interface{}); exists {\n   649→\t\t// Clean up fieldConfig defaults to match frontend behavior\n   650→\t\tif defaults, hasDefaults := fieldConfig[\"defaults\"].(map[string]interface{}); hasDefaults {\n   651→\t\t\t// Remove properties that frontend considers as defaults and omits\n   652→\t\t\tcleanupFieldConfigDefaults(defaults, panel)\n   653→\n   654→\t\t\t// Preserve custom object if it was originally present, even if empty\n   655→\t\t\tif panel[\"_originallyHadFieldConfigCustom\"] == true {\n   656→\t\t\t\tif _, hasCustom := defaults[\"custom\"]; !hasCustom {\n   657→\t\t\t\t\tdefaults[\"custom\"] = map[string]interface{}{}\n   658→\t\t\t\t}\n   659→\t\t\t}\n   660→\t\t}\n   661→\t}\n   662→}\n   663→\n   664→// isEqual checks if two values are equal (simplified version)\n   665→func isEqual(a, b interface{}) bool {\n   666→\tif a == nil && b == nil {\n   667→\t\treturn true\n   668→\t}\n   669→\tif a == nil || b == nil {\n   670→\t\treturn false\n   671→\t}\n   672→\n   673→\t// For simple types, use direct comparison\n   674→\tswitch aVal := a.(type) {\n   675→\tcase bool:\n   676→\t\tif bVal, ok := b.(bool); ok {\n   677→\t\t\treturn aVal == bVal\n   678→\t\t}\n   679→\tcase string:\n   680→\t\tif bVal, ok := b.(string); ok {\n   681→\t\t\treturn aVal == bVal\n   682→\t\t}\n   683→\tcase float64:\n   684→\t\tif bVal, ok := b.(float64); ok {\n   685→\t\t\treturn aVal == bVal\n   686→\t\t}\n   687→\tcase []interface{}:\n   688→\t\tif bVal, ok := b.([]interface{}); ok {\n   689→\t\t\tif len(aVal) != len(bVal) {\n   690→\t\t\t\treturn false\n   691→\t\t\t}\n   692→\t\t\tfor i, v := range aVal {\n   693→\t\t\t\tif !isEqual(v, bVal[i]) {\n   694→\t\t\t\t\treturn false\n   695→\t\t\t\t}\n   696→\t\t\t}\n   697→\t\t\treturn true\n   698→\t\t}\n   699→\tcase map[string]interface{}:\n   700→\t\tif bVal, ok := b.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n   701→\t\t\tif len(aVal) != len(bVal) {\n   702→\t\t\t\treturn false\n   703→\t\t\t}\n   704→\t\t\tfor k, v := range aVal {\n   705→\t\t\t\tif !isEqual(v, bVal[k]) {\n   706→\t\t\t\t\treturn false\n   707→\t\t\t\t}\n   708→\t\t\t}\n   709→\t\t\treturn true\n   710→\t\t}\n   711→\t}\n   712→\n   713→\treturn false\n   714→}\n   715→\n   716→// cleanupDashboardForSave applies the same cleanup logic as the frontend\n   717→func cleanupDashboardForSave(dashboard map[string]interface{}) {\n   718→\tremoveNonPersistedProperties(dashboard)\n   719→\tremoveNullValues(dashboard)\n   720→\tcleanupTemplating(dashboard)\n   721→\tcleanupPanels(dashboard)\n   722→\tcleanupDashboardDefaults(dashboard)\n   723→}\n   724→\n   725→// removeNonPersistedProperties removes non-persisted dashboard properties\n   726→func removeNonPersistedProperties(dashboard map[string]interface{}) {\n   727→\tnonPersistedProperties := map[string]bool{\n   728→\t\t\"events\":                           true,\n   729→\t\t\"meta\":                             true,\n   730→\t\t\"panels\":                           true, // handled specially below\n   731→\t\t\"templating\":                       true, // handled specially below\n   732→\t\t\"originalTime\":                     true,\n   733→\t\t\"originalTemplating\":               true,\n   734→\t\t\"originalLibraryPanels\":            true,\n   735→\t\t\"panelInEdit\":                      true,\n   736→\t\t\"panelInView\":                      true,\n   737→\t\t\"getVariablesFromState\":            true,\n   738→\t\t\"formatDate\":                       true,\n   739→\t\t\"appEventsSubscription\":            true,\n   740→\t\t\"panelsAffectedByVariableChange\":   true,\n   741→\t\t\"lastRefresh\":                      true,\n   742→\t\t\"timeRangeUpdatedDuringEditOrView\": true,\n   743→\t\t\"originalDashboard\":                true,\n   744→\t}\n   745→\n   746→\tfor k, v := range nonPersistedProperties {\n   747→\t\t// Do not remove \"panels\" and \"templating\" here, as they are handled specially\n   748→\t\tif (k == \"panels\" || k == \"templating\") && v {\n   749→\t\t\tcontinue\n   750→\t\t}\n   751→\t\tif v {\n   752→\t\t\tdelete(dashboard, k)\n   753→\t\t}\n   754→\t}\n   755→\n   756→\t// Remove properties that frontend omits in getSaveModel\n   757→\tdelete(dashboard, \"variables\")\n   758→}\n   759→\n   760→// removeNullValues removes null values to match frontend's JSON.stringify/parse behavior\n   761→func removeNullValues(dashboard map[string]interface{}) {\n   762→\t// This handles gnetId: null and other null properties\n   763→\tremoveIfDefaultValue(dashboard, \"gnetId\", nil)\n   764→}\n   765→\n   766→// cleanupTemplating cleans up templating to match frontend's getTemplatingSaveModel behavior\n   767→func cleanupTemplating(dashboard map[string]interface{}) {\n   768→\tif templating, ok := dashboard[\"templating\"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n   769→\t\tremoveNullValuesRecursively(templating)\n   770→\t\tcleanupTemplatingVariables(templating)\n   771→\t}\n   772→}\n   773→\n   774→// cleanupTemplatingVariables applies variable adapter logic\n   775→func cleanupTemplatingVariables(templating map[string]interface{}) {\n   776→\tif list, ok := templating[\"list\"].([]interface{}); ok {\n   777→\t\tfor _, variableInterface := range list {\n   778→\t\t\tif variable, ok := variableInterface.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n   779→\t\t\t\tcleanupVariable(variable)\n   780→\t\t\t}\n   781→\t\t}\n   782→\t}\n   783→}\n   784→\n   785→// cleanupVariable cleans up individual variable properties\n   786→func cleanupVariable(variable map[string]interface{}) {\n   787→\t// Remove null datasource\n   788→\tremoveIfDefaultValue(variable, \"datasource\", nil)\n   789→\n   790→\t// Remove properties that frontend omits in getSaveModel\n   791→\tdelete(variable, \"index\")\n   792→\n   793→\t// Apply variable type-specific logic\n   794→\tif variableType, ok := variable[\"type\"].(string); ok {\n   795→\t\tswitch variableType {\n   796→\t\tcase \"query\":\n   797→\t\t\t// Query variables: keep options: [] if refresh !== never (matches frontend getSaveModel logic)\n   798→\t\t\trefresh := schemaversion.GetIntValue(variable, \"refresh\", 1) // Default to 1 (onDashboardLoad) if not specified\n   799→\t\t\tif refresh != 0 {                                            // 0 = VariableRefreshNever\n   800→\t\t\t\tif _, hasOptions := variable[\"options\"]; !hasOptions {\n   801→\t\t\t\t\tvariable[\"options\"] = []interface{}{}\n   802→\t\t\t\t}\n   803→\t\t\t}\n   804→\t\tcase \"constant\":\n   805→\t\t\t// Constant variables: remove options completely\n   806→\t\t\tdelete(variable, \"options\")\n   807→\t\tcase \"datasource\":\n   808→\t\t\t// Datasource variables: always set options to empty array\n   809→\t\t\tvariable[\"options\"] = []interface{}{}\n   810→\t\tcase \"custom\":\n   811→\t\t\t// Custom variables: no special handling (just return rest)\n   812→\t\t\t// This is the default behavior - no additional processing needed\n   813→\t\tcase \"textbox\":\n   814→\t\t\t// Textbox variables: handle query vs originalQuery logic\n   815→\t\t\t// For now, just return rest (no special handling needed for basic cases)\n   816→\t\tcase \"adhoc\":\n   817→\t\t\t// Adhoc variables: no special handling\n   818→\t\t\t// This is the default behavior - no additional processing needed\n   819→\t\t}\n   820→\t}\n   821→}\n   822→\n   823→// cleanupPanels cleans up panels and ensures panels property always exists\n   824→func cleanupPanels(dashboard map[string]interface{}) {\n   825→\tif panels, ok := dashboard[\"panels\"].([]interface{}); ok {\n   826→\t\t// Filter out repeated panels (matches frontend getPanelSaveModels behavior)\n   827→\t\t// Frontend filters: !(panel.repeatPanelId || panel.repeatedByRow)\n   828→\t\tfilteredPanels := []interface{}{}\n   829→\t\tfor _, panelInterface := range panels {\n   830→\t\t\tif panel, ok := panelInterface.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n   831→\t\t\t\t// Skip panels with repeatPanelId or repeatedByRow\n   832→\t\t\t\tif _, hasRepeatPanelId := panel[\"repeatPanelId\"]; hasRepeatPanelId {\n   833→\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n   834→\t\t\t\t}\n   835→\t\t\t\tif _, hasRepeatedByRow := panel[\"repeatedByRow\"]; hasRepeatedByRow {\n   836→\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n   837→\t\t\t\t}\n   838→\t\t\t\tfilteredPanels = append(filteredPanels, panel)\n   839→\t\t\t}\n   840→\t\t}\n   841→\n   842→\t\tcleanupPanelList(filteredPanels)\n   843→\t\tsortPanelsByGridPosition(filteredPanels)\n   844→\t\tdashboard[\"panels\"] = filteredPanels\n   845→\t} else {\n   846→\t\t// Ensure panels property exists even if empty (matches frontend behavior)\n   847→\t\tdashboard[\"panels\"] = []interface{}{}\n   848→\t}\n   849→}\n   850→\n   851→// cleanupPanelList cleans up all panels including nested ones\n   852→func cleanupPanelList(panels []interface{}) {\n   853→\tfor _, panelInterface := range panels {\n   854→\t\tif panel, ok := panelInterface.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n   855→\t\t\tcleanupPanelForSaveWithContext(panel, false)\n   856→\n   857→\t\t\t// Handle nested panels in row panels\n   858→\t\t\tif nestedPanels, ok := panel[\"panels\"].([]interface{}); ok {\n   859→\t\t\t\tfor _, nestedPanelInterface := range nestedPanels {\n   860→\t\t\t\t\tif nestedPanel, ok := nestedPanelInterface.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n   861→\t\t\t\t\t\tcleanupPanelForSaveWithContext(nestedPanel, true)\n   862→\t\t\t\t\t}\n   863→\t\t\t\t}\n   864→\t\t\t}\n   865→\t\t}\n   866→\t}\n   867→}\n   868→\n   869→// sortPanelsByGridPosition sorts panels by grid position (matches frontend sortPanelsByGridPos behavior)\n   870→func sortPanelsByGridPosition(panels []interface{}) {\n   871→\tsort.SliceStable(panels, func(i, j int) bool {\n   872→\t\tpanelA, okA := panels[i].(map[string]interface{})\n   873→\t\tpanelB, okB := panels[j].(map[string]interface{})\n   874→\t\tif !okA || !okB {\n   875→\t\t\treturn false\n   876→\t\t}\n   877→\n   878→\t\t// Get gridPos or use default values if missing\n   879→\t\tgridPosA, okA := panelA[\"gridPos\"].(map[string]interface{})\n   880→\t\tgridPosB, okB := panelB[\"gridPos\"].(map[string]interface{})\n   881→\n   882→\t\t// Default gridPos values (matches frontend PanelModel defaults)\n   883→\t\tdefaultY := float64(0)\n   884→\t\tdefaultX := float64(0)\n   885→\n   886→\t\tyA := defaultY\n   887→\t\tif okA {\n   888→\t\t\tif y, ok := gridPosA[\"y\"].(float64); ok {\n   889→\t\t\t\tyA = y\n   890→\t\t\t} else if y, ok := gridPosA[\"y\"].(int); ok {\n   891→\t\t\t\tyA = float64(y)\n   892→\t\t\t}\n   893→\t\t}\n   894→\n   895→\t\tyB := defaultY\n   896→\t\tif okB {\n   897→\t\t\tif y, ok := gridPosB[\"y\"].(float64); ok {\n   898→\t\t\t\tyB = y\n   899→\t\t\t} else if y, ok := gridPosB[\"y\"].(int); ok {\n   900→\t\t\t\tyB = float64(y)\n   901→\t\t\t}\n   902→\t\t}\n   903→\n   904→\t\tif yA == yB {\n   905→\t\t\txA := defaultX\n   906→\t\t\tif okA {\n   907→\t\t\t\tif x, ok := gridPosA[\"x\"].(float64); ok {\n   908→\t\t\t\t\txA = x\n   909→\t\t\t\t} else if x, ok := gridPosA[\"x\"].(int); ok {\n   910→\t\t\t\t\txA = float64(x)\n   911→\t\t\t\t}\n   912→\t\t\t}\n   913→\n   914→\t\t\txB := defaultX\n   915→\t\t\tif okB {\n   916→\t\t\t\tif x, ok := gridPosB[\"x\"].(float64); ok {\n   917→\t\t\t\t\txB = x\n   918→\t\t\t\t} else if x, ok := gridPosB[\"x\"].(int); ok {\n   919→\t\t\t\t\txB = float64(x)\n   920→\t\t\t\t}\n   921→\t\t\t}\n   922→\t\t\treturn xA < xB\n   923→\t\t}\n   924→\t\treturn yA < yB\n   925→\t})\n   926→}\n   927→\n   928→// cleanupRowPanelProperties removes default row panel properties that frontend filters out\n   929→func cleanupRowPanelProperties(panel map[string]interface{}) {\n   930→\tpanelType, ok := panel[\"type\"].(string)\n   931→\tif !ok || panelType != \"row\" {\n   932→\t\treturn\n   933→\t}\n   934→\n   935→\t// Remove repeat if empty string (default value)\n   936→\tremoveIfDefaultValue(panel, \"repeat\", \"\")\n   937→}\n   938→\n   939→// applyPanelAutoMigration applies the same auto-migration logic as the frontend PanelModel constructor\n   940→func applyPanelAutoMigration(panel map[string]interface{}) {\n   941→\tpanelType, ok := panel[\"type\"].(string)\n   942→\tif !ok {\n   943→\t\treturn\n   944→\t}\n   945→\n   946→\tvar newType string\n   947→\n   948→\t// Graph needs special logic as it can be migrated to multiple panels\n   949→\t// Including graphite which was previously migrated to graph in the schema version 2 migration in DashboardMigrator.ts\n   950→\t// but this was a bug because in there graphite was set to graph, but since those migrations run\n   951→\t// after PanelModel.restoreModel where autoMigrateFrom is set, this caused the graph migration to be skipped.\n   952→\t// And this resulted in a dashboard with invalid panels.\n   953→\tif panelType == \"graph\" || panelType == \"graphite\" {\n   954→\t\t// Check xaxis mode for special cases\n   955→\t\tnewType = getGraphAutoMigration(panel)\n   956→\t} else {\n   957→\t\t// Check autoMigrateAngular mapping\n   958→\t\tautoMigrateAngular := map[string]string{\n   959→\t\t\t\"table-old\":                \"table\",\n   960→\t\t\t\"singlestat\":               \"stat\",\n   961→\t\t\t\"grafana-singlestat-panel\": \"stat\",\n   962→\t\t\t\"grafana-piechart-panel\":   \"piechart\",\n   963→\t\t\t\"grafana-worldmap-panel\":   \"geomap\",\n   964→\t\t\t\"natel-discrete-panel\":     \"state-timeline\",\n   965→\t\t}\n   966→\n   967→\t\tif mappedType, exists := autoMigrateAngular[panelType]; exists {\n   968→\t\t\tnewType = mappedType\n   969→\t\t}\n   970→\t}\n   971→\n   972→\t// Apply auto-migration if a new type was determined\n   973→\tif newType != \"\" {\n   974→\t\tpanel[\"autoMigrateFrom\"] = panelType\n   975→\t\tpanel[\"type\"] = newType\n   976→\t}\n   977→}\n   978→\n   979→func getGraphAutoMigration(panel map[string]interface{}) string {\n   980→\tnewType := \"\"\n   981→\tif xaxis, ok := panel[\"xaxis\"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n   982→\t\tif mode, ok := xaxis[\"mode\"].(string); ok {\n   983→\t\t\tswitch mode {\n   984→\t\t\tcase \"series\":\n   985→\t\t\t\t// Check legend values for bargauge\n   986→\t\t\t\tif legend, ok := panel[\"legend\"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n   987→\t\t\t\t\tif values, ok := legend[\"values\"].(bool); ok && values {\n   988→\t\t\t\t\t\tnewType = \"bargauge\"\n   989→\t\t\t\t\t} else {\n   990→\t\t\t\t\t\tnewType = \"barchart\"\n   991→\t\t\t\t\t}\n   992→\t\t\t\t} else {\n   993→\t\t\t\t\tnewType = \"barchart\"\n   994→\t\t\t\t}\n   995→\t\t\tcase \"histogram\":\n   996→\t\t\t\tnewType = \"histogram\"\n   997→\t\t\t}\n   998→\t\t}\n   999→\t}\n  1000→\n  1001→\t// Default graph migration to timeseries\n  1002→\tif newType == \"\" {\n  1003→\t\tnewType = \"timeseries\"\n  1004→\t}\n  1005→\n  1006→\treturn newType\n  1007→}\n  1008→\n  1009→// removeNullValuesRecursively removes null values from nested objects and arrays\n  1010→// This matches the frontend's JSON.stringify/parse behavior\n  1011→func removeNullValuesRecursively(data interface{}) {\n  1012→\tremoveNullValuesRecursivelyWithContext(data, \"\")\n  1013→}\n  1014→\n  1015→// removeNullValuesRecursivelyWithContext removes null values from nested objects and arrays\n  1016→// This matches the frontend's JSON.stringify/parse behavior in getSaveModelClone()\n  1017→func removeNullValuesRecursivelyWithContext(data interface{}, panelType string) {\n  1018→\tswitch v := data.(type) {\n  1019→\tcase map[string]interface{}:\n  1020→\t\t// Remove null values from map\n  1021→\t\tfor key, value := range v {\n  1022→\t\t\tif value == nil {\n  1023→\t\t\t\t// Frontend removes null values via JSON serialization, so we should too\n  1024→\t\t\t\t// No special case needed for threshold steps\n  1025→\t\t\t\tdelete(v, key)\n  1026→\t\t\t} else {\n  1027→\t\t\t\t// Recursively process nested values\n  1028→\t\t\t\tremoveNullValuesRecursivelyWithContext(value, panelType)\n  1029→\t\t\t}\n  1030→\t\t}\n  1031→\tcase []interface{}:\n  1032→\t\t// Process array elements\n  1033→\t\tfor _, item := range v {\n  1034→\t\t\tif item != nil {\n  1035→\t\t\t\tremoveNullValuesRecursivelyWithContext(item, panelType)\n  1036→\t\t\t}\n  1037→\t\t}\n  1038→\t}\n  1039→}\n  1040→\n  1041→// removeIfDefaultValue removes the key from the map if its value equals the defaultValue\n  1042→func removeIfDefaultValue(data map[string]interface{}, key string, defaultValue interface{}) {\n  1043→\tif val, ok := data[key]; ok && isEqual(val, defaultValue) {\n  1044→\t\tdelete(data, key)\n  1045→\t}\n  1046→}\n  1047→\n  1048→// cleanupDashboardDefaults removes dashboard-level default values that frontend filters out\n  1049→func cleanupDashboardDefaults(dashboard map[string]interface{}) {\n  1050→\t// Remove style if it's the default \"dark\" value\n  1051→\tremoveIfDefaultValue(dashboard, \"style\", \"dark\")\n  1052→\n  1053→\t// Remove hideControls if it's the default false value\n  1054→\tremoveIfDefaultValue(dashboard, \"hideControls\", false)\n  1055→\n  1056→\t// Remove dashboard id if it's null\n  1057→\tremoveIfDefaultValue(dashboard, \"id\", nil)\n  1058→\n  1059→\t// Remove version property - it's managed by the backend metadata, not the spec\n  1060→\tdelete(dashboard, \"version\")\n  1061→\n  1062→\t// Remove transient properties that frontend filters out during getSaveModelClone()\n  1063→\t// These properties are lost during frontend's property copying loop in getSaveModelCloneOld()\n  1064→\tdelete(dashboard, \"iteration\") // Template variable iteration timestamp\n  1065→\tdelete(dashboard, \"nav\")       // Removed after V7 migration\n  1066→\tdelete(dashboard, \"pulldowns\") // Removed after V6 migration - frontend doesn't have this property\n  1067→}\n  1068→\n  1069→// cleanupFieldConfigDefaults removes properties that frontend considers as defaults and omits\n  1070→func cleanupFieldConfigDefaults(defaults map[string]interface{}, panel map[string]interface{}) {\n 65→standardEditorsRegistry.setInit(getAllOptionEditors);\n    66→standardFieldConfigEditorRegistry.setInit(getAllStandardFieldConfigs);\n    67→\n    68→type PanelSchemeUpgradeHandler = (panel: PanelModel) => PanelModel;\n    69→\n    70→/**\n    71→ * The current version of the dashboard schema.\n    72→ *\n    73→ * NOTE: Schema version 42 is the FINAL version for the v1 dashboard API.\n    74→ * DO NOT increment this number or add new schema migrations.\n    75→ *\n    76→ * This is necessary due to the migration of the legacy dashboards API to the app platform.\n    77→ *\n    78→ * For panel-specific migrations, implement them as panel migrations in the\n    79→ * individual panel plugin migration handlers instead of adding new schema versions.\n    80→ *\n    81→ * Legacy migration instructions (for reference only):\n    82→ * To add a dashboard migration increment this number\n    83→ * and then add your migration at the bottom of 'updateSchema'\n    84→ * hint: search \"Add migration here\"\n    85→ *\n    86→ * This number also needs to be updated on the CUE schema:\n    87→ * kinds/dashboard/dashboard_kind.cue\n    88→ * Example PR: #87712\n    89→ */\n    90→export const DASHBOARD_SCHEMA_VERSION = 42;\n    91→export class DashboardMigrator {\n    92→  dashboard: DashboardModel;\n    93→\n    94→  constructor(dashboardModel: DashboardModel) {\n    95→    this.dashboard = dashboardModel;\n    96→\n    97→    // for tests to pass\n    98→    if (!getDataSourceSrv()) {\n    99→      setDataSourceSrv(new DatasourceSrv());\n   100→    }\n   101→  }\n   102→\n   103→  updateSchema(old: any, targetSchemaVersion?: number) {\n   104→    let i, j, k, n;\n   105→    const oldVersion = this.dashboard.schemaVersion;\n   106→    const panelUpgrades: PanelSchemeUpgradeHandler[] = [];\n   107→    const finalTargetVersion = targetSchemaVersion || DASHBOARD_SCHEMA_VERSION;\n   108→\n   109→    if (oldVersion === finalTargetVersion) {\n   110→      return;\n   111→    }\n   112→\n   113→    // version 2 schema changes\n   114→    if (oldVersion < 2 && finalTargetVersion >= 2) {\n   115→      if (old.services) {\n   116→        if (old.services.filter) {\n   117→          this.dashboard.time = old.services.filter.time;\n   118→          this.dashboard.templating.list = old.services.filter.list || [];\n   119→        }\n   120→      }\n   121→\n   122→      // we used to have graphite panel type migration logic here\n   123→      // but this is handled by auto migration, see public/app/features/dashboard/state/getPanelPluginToMigrateTo.ts\n   124→    }\n   125→\n   126→    // schema version 3 changes\n   127→    if (oldVersion < 3 && finalTargetVersion >= 3) {\n   128→      // Panel ID assignment is now handled by ensurePanelsHaveUniqueIds() in DashboardModel\n   129→      // and the grid layout migration properly handles panels without IDs in rows\n   130→    }\n   131→\n   132→    // schema version 4 changes\n   133→    if (oldVersion < 4 && finalTargetVersion >= 4) {\n   134→      // graph migration is handled through the auto migration\n   135→      // see schema\n   140→      const annotations = find(old.pulldowns, { type: 'annotations' });\n   141→\n   142→      if (annotations) {\n   143→        this.dashboard.annotations = {\n   144→          list: annotations.annotations || [],\n   145→        };\n   146→      }\n   147→\n   148→      // update template variables\n   149→      for (i = 0; i < this.dashboard.templating.list.length; i++) {\n   150→        const variable = this.dashboard.templating.list[i];\n   151→        if (variable.datasource === void 0) {\n   152→          variable.datasource = null;\n   153→        }\n   154→        if (variable.type === 'filter') {\n   155→          variable.type = 'query';\n   156→        }\n   157→        if (variable.type === void 0) {\n   158→          variable.type = 'query';\n   159→        }\n   160→        if (variable.allFormat === void 0) {\n   161→          delete variable.allFormat;\n   162→        }\n   163→      }\n   164→    }\n   165→\n   166→    if (oldVersion < 7 && finalTargetVersion >= 7) {\n   167→      if (old.nav && old.nav.length) {\n   168→        this.dashboard.timepicker = old.nav[0];\n   169→      }\n   170→    }\n   171→\n   172→    if (oldVersion < 8 && finalTargetVersion >= 8) {\n   173→      panelUpgrades.push((panel: any) => {\n   174→        each(panel.targets, (target) => {\n   175→          // update old influxdb query schema\n   176→          if (target.fields && target.tags && target.groupBy) {\n   177→            if (target.rawQuery) {\n   178→              delete target.fields;\n   179→              delete target.fill;\n   180→            } else {\n   181→              target.select = map(target.fields, (field) => {\n   182→                const parts = [];\n   183→                parts.push({ type: 'field', params: [field.name] });\n   184→                parts.push({ type: field.func, params: [] });\n   185→                if (field.mathExpr) {\n   186→                  parts.push({ type: 'math', params: [field.mathExpr] });\n   187→                }\n   188→                if (field.asExpr) {\n   189→                  parts.push({ type: 'alias', params: [field.asExpr] });\n   190→                }\n   191→                return parts;\n   192→              });\n   193→              delete target.fields;\n   194→              each(target.groupBy, (part) => {\n   195→                if (part.type === 'time' && part.interval) {\n   196→                  part.params = [part.interval];\n   197→                  delete part.interval;\n   198→                }\n   199→                if (part.type === 'tag' && part.key) {\n   200→                  part.params = [part.key];\n   201→                  delete part.key;\n   202→                }\n   203→              });\n   204→\n   205→              if (target.fill) {\n   206→                target.groupBy.push({ type: 'fill', params: [target.fill] });\n   207→                delete target.fill;\n   208→              }\n   209→            }\n   210→          }\n   211→        });\n   212→\n   213→        return panel;\n   214→      });\n   215→    }\n   216→\n   217→    // schema version 9 changes\n   218→    if (oldVersion < 9 && finalTargetVersion >= 9) {\n   219→      // singlestat panel is automigrated to stat panel\n   220→      // see autoMigrateAngular map in public/app/features/dashboard/state/PanelModel.ts\n   221→    }\n   222→\n   223→    // schema version 10 changes\n   224→    if (oldVersion < 10 && finalTargetVersion >= 10) {\n   225→      // move aliasYAxis changes\n   226→      panelUpgrades.push((panel: (oldVersion < 17 && finalTargetVersion >= 17) {\n   276→      panelUpgrades.push((panel: any) => {\n   277→        if (panel.minSpan) {\n   278→          const max = GRID_COLUMN_COUNT / panel.minSpan;\n   279→          const factors = getFactors(GRID_COLUMN_COUNT);\n   280→          // find the best match compared to factors\n   281→          // (ie.           }) - 1\n   287→            ];\n   288→        }\n   289→\n   290→        delete panel.minSpan;\n   291→\n   292→        return panel;\n   293→      });\n   294→    }\n   295→\n   296→    if 304→            decimals: panel.options.decimals,\n   305→            prefix: panel.options.prefix,\n   306→            suffix: panel.options.suffix,\n   307→          };\n   308→\n   309→          //         delete panel.options.prefix;\n   320→          delete panel.options.suffix;\n   321→          delete panel['options-gauge'];\n   322→        }\n   323→\n   324→        return panel;\n   325→      });\n   326→    }\n   327→\n   328→    if (oldVersion < 19 && finalTargetVersion >= 19) {\n   329→      // migrate change to gauge options\n   330→      panelUpgrades.push((panel: any) => {\n 337→    }\n   338→\n   339→    if (oldVersion < 20 && finalTargetVersion >= 20) {\n   340→      const updateLinks = (link: DataLink) => {\n   341→        return {\n   342→          ...link,\n   343→ object for configuration\n   644→    if (oldVersion < 38 && finalTargetVersion >= 38) {\n   645→      panelUpgrades.push((panel: PanelModel) => {\n   646→        if (panel.type === 'table' && 663→                if (override.properties[j].id === 'custom.displayMode') {\n   664→                  override.properties[j].id = 'custom.cellOptions';\n   665→                  override.properties[j].value = migrateTableDisplayModeToCellOptions(overrideDisplayMode);\n   666→                }\n   667→              }\n   668→            }\n   669→          }\n   670→        }\n   671→\n   672→        return panel;\n   673→      });\n   674→    }\n   675→\n   676→    // Update the configuration of the Timeseries to table transformation\n   677→    // to support multiple options per query\n   678→    if (oldVersion < 39 && finalTargetVersion >= 39) {\n   679→      panelUpgrades.push((panel: PanelModel) => {\n   680→        panel.transformations?.forEach((transformation) => {\n   681→          // If we run into a timeSeriesTable transformation\n   682→          // and it doesn't have undefined options then we migrate\n   683→          if (\n   684→            transformation.id === 'timeSeriesTable' &&\n   685→            transformation.options !== undefined &&\n   686→            transformation.options.refIdToStat !== undefined\n   687→          ) {\n   688→            let tableTransformOptions: TimeSeriesTableTransformerOptions = {};\n   689→\n   690→            // For each {refIdtoStat} record which maps refId to a statistic\n   691→            // we add that to the stat property of the new\n   692→            // RefIdTransformerOptions interface which includes multiple settings\n   693→            for (const [refId, stat] of Object.entries(transformation.options.refIdToStat)) {\n   694→              let newSettings: RefIdTransformerOptions = {};\n   695→              // In this case the easiest way is just to do a type\n   696→              // assertion as iterated entries have unknown types\n   697→              newSettings.stat = stat as ReducerID;\n   698→              tableTransformOptions[refId] = newSettings;\n   699→            }\n   700→\n   701→            // Update the options\n   702→            transformation.options = tableTransformOptions;\n   703→          }\n   704→        });\n   705→\n   706→        return panel;\n   707→      });\n   708→    }\n   709→\n   710→    if (oldVersion < 40 && finalTargetVersion >= 40) {\n   711→      // In old dashboards refresh property can be a boolean\n   712→      if (typeof this.dashboard.refresh !== 'string') {\n   713→        this.dashboard.refresh = '';\n   714→      }\n   715→    }\n   716→\n   717→    if (oldVersion < 41 && finalTargetVersion >= 41) {\n   718→      // time_options is a legacy property that was not used since grafana version 5\n   719→      //  therefore deprecating this property from the schema\n   720→      if ('time_options' in this.dashboard.timepicker) {\n   721→        delete this.dashboard.timepicker.time_options;\n   722→      }\n   723→    }\n   724→\n   725→    if (oldVersion < 42 && finalTargetVersion >= 42) {\n   726→      panelUpgrades.push(migrateHideFromFunctionality);\n   727→    }\n   728→\n   729→    /**\n   730→     * ⚠️  WARNING: DO NOT ADD NEW MIGRATIONS HERE ⚠️\n   731→     *\n   732→     * Schema version 42 is the FINAL version for the v1 dashboard API.\n   733→     * This is due to the migration of the legacy dashboards API to the app platform.\n   734→     *\n   735→     * For panel-specific migrations, implement them as panel migrations in the\n   736→     * individual panel plugin migration handlers instead of adding new schema versions.\n   737→     */\n   738→\n   739→    // Apply panel upgrades if any exist\n   740→    if (panelUpgrades.length > 0) {\n   741→      for (j = 0; j < this.dashboard.panels.length; j++) {\n   742→        for (k = 0; k < panelUpgrades.length; k++) {\n   743→          this.dashboard.panels[j] = panelUpgrades[k].call(this, this.dashboard.panels[j]);\n   744→          const rowPanels = this.dashboard.panels[j].panels;\n   745→          if (rowPanels) {\n   746→            for (n = 0; n < rowPanels.length; n++) {\n   747→              rowPanels[n] = panelUpgrades[k].call(this, rowPanels[n]);\n   748→            }\n   749→          }\n   750→        }\n   751→      }\n   752→    }\n   753→\n   754→    // Always update schema version after migrations, regardless of panel upgrades\n   755→    // Only update schema version if migrations were actually needed\n   756→    if (oldVersion < finalTargetVersion) {\n   757→      this.dashboard.schemaVersion = finalTargetVersion;\n   758→    }\n   759→  }\n   760→\n   761→  private removeRepeatedPanels() {\n   762→    const newPanels = [];\n   763→\n   764→    for (const panel of this.dashboard.panels) {\n   765→      // @ts-expect-error\n   766→      if (panel.repeatPanelId || panel.repeatByRow) {\n   767→        continue;\n   768→      }\n   769→\n   770→      // Filter out repeats in collapsed rows\n   771→      if (panel.type === 'row' && Array.isArray(panel.panels)) {\n   772→        panel.panels = panel.panels.filter((x) => !x.repeatPanelId);\n   773→      }\n   774→\n   775→ statistic.\n   782→  // E.g query.statistics = ['Max', 'Min'] will be migrated to two queries - query1.statistic = 'Max' and query2.statistic = 'Min'\n   783→  // New queries, that were created during migration, are put at the end of the array.\n   784→  migrateCloudWatchQueries(panel: PanelModel) {\n   785→    for (const target of panel.targets || []) {\n   786→      if (isCloudWatchQuery(target)) panel.targets.push(newQuery);\n   793→          }\n   794→        }\n   795→      }\n   796→    }\n   797→  }\n   798→\n   799→  // Migrates CloudWatch annotation queries that use multiple statistics into separate queries.\n   800→  // For example, if an annotation query uses ['Max', 'Min'] statistics, it will be split into\n   801→  // two separate annotation queries - one with 'Max' and another with 'Min'.\n   802→  // The new annotation queries are added to the end of the annotations list.\n   803→  migrateCloudWatchAnnotationQuery() {\n   804→    for (const annotation of 807→        for (const newAnnotationQuery of newAnnotationQueries) {\n   808→          this.dashboard.annotations.list.push(newAnnotationQuery);\n   809→        }\n   810→      }\n   811→    }\n   812→  }\n   813→\n   814→  upgradeToGridLayout(old: any) {\n   815→    let yPos = 0;\n   816→    const widthFactor = GRID_COLUMN_COUNT / 12;\n   817→\n   818→    const maxPanelId =\n   819→      max(\n   820→        flattenDeep(\n   821→          map(old.rows, (row) => {\n   822→            return map(row.panels, 'id');\n   823→          })\n   824→        ).filter((id) => id != null)\n   825→      ) || 0;\n   826→    let nextRowId = maxPanelId + 1;\n   827→\n   828→    if (!old.rows) {\n   829→      return;\n   830→    }\n   831→\n   832→    // Add special \"row\" panels if even one row is collapsed, repeated or has visible title\n   833→    const showRows = some(old.rows, (row) => row.collapse || row.showTitle || row.repeat);\n   834→\n   835→    for (const row of old.rows) {\n   836→      if (row.repeatIteration) {\n   837→        continue;\n   838→      }\n   839→\n   840→      const height = row.height || DEFAULT_ROW_HEIGHT;\n   841→      const rowGridHeight = getGridHeight(height);\n   842→\n   843→      const rowPanel: any = {};\n   844→      let rowPanelModel: PanelModel | undefined;\n   845→\n   846→      if (showRows) {\n   847→        // add special row panel\n   848→        rowPanel.id = nextRowId;\n   849→        rowPanel.type = 'row';\n   850→        rowPanel.title = row.title;\n   851→        rowPanel.collapsed = row.collapse;\n   852→        rowPanel.repeat = row.repeat;\n   853→        rowPanel.panels = [];\n   854→        rowPanel.gridPos = {\n   855→          x: 0,\n   856→          y: yPos,\n   857→          w: GRID_COLUMN_COUNT,\n   858→          h: rowGridHeight,\n   859→        };\n   860→        rowPanelModel = new PanelModel(rowPanel);\n   861→        nextRowId++;\n   862→        yPos++;\n   863→      }\n   864→\n   865→      const rowArea = new RowArea(rowGridHeight, GRID_COLUMN_COUNT, yPos);\n   866→\n   867→      for (const panel of row.panels) {\n   868→        panel.span = panel.span || DEFAULT_PANEL_SPAN;\n   869→        if (panel.minSpan) {\n   870→          panel.minSpan = Math.min(GRID_COLUMN_COUNT, (GRID_COLUMN_COUNT / 12) * panel.minSpan);\n   871→        }\n   872→        const panelWidth = Math.floor(panel.span) * widthFactor;\n   873→        const panelHeight = panel.height ? getGridHeight(panel.height) : rowGridHeight;\n   874→\n   875→        const panelPos = rowArea.getPanelPosition(panelHeight, panelWidth);\n   876→        yPos = rowArea.yPos;\n   877→        panel.gridPos = {\n   878→          x: panelPos.x,\n   879→          y: yPos + panelPos.y,\n   880→          w: panelWidth,\n   881→          h: panelHeight,\n   882→        };\n   883→        rowArea.addPanel(panel.gridPos);\n   884→\n   885→        delete panel.span;\n   886→\n   887→      }\n   892→      }\n   893→\n   894→      if (rowPanelModel) {\n   895→        this.dashboard.panels.push(rowPanelModel);\n   896→      }\n   897→\n   898→      if (!(rowPanelModel && rowPanel.collapsed)) {\n   899→        yPos += rowGridHeight;\n   900→      }\n   901→    }\n   902→  }\n   903→}\n   904→\n   905→function getGridHeight(height: number | string) {\n   906→  if (isString(height)) {\n   907→    height = parseInt(height.replace('px', ''), 10);\n   908→  }\n   909→\n   910→  if (height < MIN_PANEL_HEIGHT) {\n   911→    height = MIN_PANEL_HEIGHT;\n   912→  }\n   913→\n   914→  const gridHeight = Math.ceil(height / (GRID_CELL_HEIGHT + GRID_CELL_VMARGIN));\n   915→  return gridHeight;\n   916→}\n   917→\n   918→/**\n   919→ * RowArea represents dashboard row filled by panels\n   920→ * area is an array of numbers represented filled column's cells like\n   921→ *  -----------------------\n   922→ * |******** ****\n   923→ * |******** ****\n   924→ * |********\n   925→ *  -----------------------\n   926→ *  33333333 2222 00000 ...\n   927→ */\n   928→class RowArea {\n   929→  area: number[];\n   930→  yPos: number;\n   931→  height: number;\n   932→\n   933→  constructor(height: number, width = GRID_COLUMN_COUNT, rowYPos = 0) {\n   934→    this.area = new Array(width).fill(0);\n   935→    this.yPos = rowYPos;\n   936→    this.height = height;\n   937→  }\n   938→\n   939→  reset() {\n   940→    this.area.fill(0);\n   941→  }\n   942→\n   943→  /**\n   944→   * Update area after adding the panel.\n   945→   */\n   946→  addPanel(gridPos: any) {\n   947→    for (let i = gridPos.x; i < gridPos.x + gridPos.w; i++) {\n   948→      if (!this.area[i] || gridPos.y + gridPos.h - this.yPos > this.area[i]) {\n   949→        this.area[i] = gridPos.y + gridPos.h - this.yPos;\n   950→      }\n   951→    }\n   952→    return this.area;\n   953→  }\n   954→\n   955→  /**\n   956→   * Calculate position for the new panel in the row.\n   957→   */\n   958→  getPanelPosition(panelHeight: number, panelWidth: number, callOnce = false): any {\n   959→    let startPlace, endPlace;\n   960→    let place;\n   961→    for (let i = this.area.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {\n   962→      if (this.height - this.area[i] > 0) {\n   963→        if (endPlace === undefined) {\n   964→          endPlace = i;\n   965→        } else {\n   966→          if (i < this.area.length - 1 && this.area[i] <= this.area[i + 1]) {\n   967→            startPlace = i;\n   968→          } else {\n   969→            break;\n   970→          }\n   971→        }\n   972→      } else {\n   973→        break;\n   974→      }\n   975→    }\n   976→\n   977→    if (startPlace !== undefined && endPlace !== undefined && endPlace - startPlace >= panelWidth - 1) {\n   978→      const yPos = max(this.area.slice(startPlace));\n   979→      place = {\n   980→        x: startPlace,\n   981→        y: yPos,\n   982→      };\n   983→    } else if (!callOnce) {\n   984→      // wrap to next row\n   985→      this.yPos += this.height;\n   986→      this.reset();\n   987→      return this.getPanelPosition(panelHeight, panelWidth, true);\n   988→    } else {\n   989→      return null;\n   990→    }\n   991→\n   992→    return place;\n   993→  }\n   994→}\n   995→\n   996→function upgradePanelLink(link: any): DataLink {\n   997→  let url = link.url;\n   998→\n   999→  if (!url && link.dashboard) {\n  1000→    url = `dashboard/db/${kbn.slugifyForUrl(link.dashboard)}`;\n  1001→  }\n  1002→\n  1003→  if (!url && link.dashUri) {\n  1004→    url = 1023→\n  1024→  return {\n  1025→    url: url,\n  1026→    title: link.title,\n  1027→    targetBlank: link.targetBlank,\n  1028→  };\n  1029→}\n  1030→\n  1031→function updateVariablesSyntax(text: 1054→interface MigrateDatasourceNameOptions {\n  1055→  returnDefaultAsNull: boolean;\n  1056→}\n  1057→\n  1058→export function migrateDatasourceNameToRef(\n  1059→  nameOrRef: string | DataSourceRef 1559→\t\t})\n  1560→\t}\n  1561→}\n  1562→\n  1563→// Helper function to compare values recursively\n  1564→func compareValues(actual, expected interface{}) bool {\n  1565→\tif actual == nil && expected == nil {\n  1566→\t\treturn true\n  1567→\t}\n  1568→\tif actual == nil || expected == nil {\n  1569→\t\treturn false\n  1570→\t}\n  1571→\n  1572→\tactualMap, actualOk := actual.(map[string]interface{})\n  1573→\texpectedMap, expectedOk := expected.(map[string]interface{})\n  1574→\n  1575→\tif actualOk && expectedOk {\n  1576→\t\tif len(actualMap) != len(expectedMap) {\n  1577→\t\t\treturn false\n  1578→\t\t}\n  1579→\t\tfor key, expectedValue := range expectedMap {\n  1580→\t\t\tactualValue, exists := actualMap[key]\n  1581→\t\t\tif !exists || !compareValues(actualValue, expectedValue) {\n  1582→\t\t\t\treturn false\n  1583→\t\t\t}\n  1584→\t\t}\n  1585→\t\treturn true\n  1586→\t}\n  1587→\n  1588→\tactualSlice, actualSliceOk := actual.([]interface{})\n  1589→\texpectedSlice, expectedSliceOk := expected.([]interface{})\n  1590→\n  1591→\tif actualSliceOk && expectedSliceOk {\n  1592→\t\tif len(actualSlice) != len(expectedSlice) {\n  1593→\t\t\treturn false\n  1594→\t\t}\n  1595→\t\tfor i, expectedValue := range expectedSlice {\n  1596→\t\t\tif !compareValues(actualSlice[i], expectedValue) {\n  1597→\t\t\t\treturn false\n  1598→\t\t\t}\n  1599→\t\t}\n  1600→\t\treturn true\n  1601→\t}\n  1602→\n  1603→\treturn actual == expected\n  1604→}\n  1605→\n  1606→// Helper function to deep copy interface{}\n  1607→func deepCopy(src interface{}) interface{} {\n  1608→\tswitch v := src.(type) {\n  1609→\tcase map[string]interface{}:\n  1610→\t\tdst := make(map[string]interface{})\n  1611→\t\tfor k, val := range v {\n  1612→\t\t\tdst[k] = deepCopy(val)\n  1613→\t\t}\n  1614→\t\treturn dst\n  1615→\tcase []interface{}:\n  1616→\t\tdst := make([]interface{}, len(v))\n  1617→\t\tfor i, val := range v {\n  1618→\t\t\tdst[i] = deepCopy(val)\n  1619→\t\t}\n  1620→\t\treturn dst\n  1621→\tdefault:\n  1622→\t\treturn src\n  1623→\t}\n  1624→}\n  1625→\n  1626→func {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WAcTqfWSkcKpUKNfgmBczq","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→# Dashboard migrations\n     2→\n     3→This document describes the Grafana dashboard migration system, focusing on conversion-level practices including metrics, logging, and testing infrastructure for API version conversions. For schema version migration implementation details, see the [SchemaVersion Migration Guide](./schemaversion/README.md).\n     4→\n     5→## Table of Contents\n     6→\n     7→- [Overview](#overview)\n     8→  - [Conversion Flow](#conversion-flow-v0--v1--v2)\n     9→  - [v0 to v1 Conversion](#v0-to-v1-conversion)\n    10→  - [v1 to v2 Conversion](#v1-to-v2-conversion)\n    11→- [Conversion Matrix](#conversion-matrix)\n    12→- [API Versions](#api-versions)\n    13→- [Schema Versions](#schema-versions)\n    14→- [Testing](#testing)\n    15→  - [Backend conversion tests](#backend-conversion-tests)\n    16→  - [Frontend migration comparison tests](#frontend-migration-comparison-tests)\n    17→- [Monitoring Migrations](#monitoring-migrations)\n    18→  - [Metrics](#metrics)\n    19→    - [Dashboard conversion success metric](#1-dashboard-conversion-success-metric)\n    20→    - [Dashboard conversion failure metric](#2-dashboard-conversion-failure-metric)\n    21→  - [Error Types](#error-types)\n    22→  - [Logging](#logging)\n    23→    - [Log structure](#log-structure)\n    24→    - [Log levels](#log-levels)\n    25→  - [Implementation Details](#implementation-details)\n    26→    - [Automatic instrumentation](#automatic-instrumentation)\n    27→    - [Error handling](#error-handling)\n    28→  - [Registration](#registration)\n    29→    - [Metrics registration](#metrics-registration)\n    30→    - [Available metrics](#available-metrics)\n    31→- [Related Documentation](#related-documentation)\n    32→\n    33→## Overview\n    34→\n    35→The Grafana dashboard migration system operates across three main conversion layers:\n    36→\n    37→### Conversion Flow: v0 → v1 → v2\n    38→\n    39→```\n    40→v0alpha1 (Legacy JSON) → v1beta1 (Migrated JSON) → v2alpha1/v2beta1 (Structured)\n    41→```\n    42→\n    43→#### v0 to v1 Conversion:\n    44→- All schema migrations (v0-v42) are executed in the backend\n    45→- Ports the logic from DashboardMigrator and implements built-in plugin migrations for panel plugins since backend cannot load plugins\n    46→- Transforms legacy JSON dashboards to migrated JSON format\n    47→- Handles backward compatibility for older dashboard formats\n    48→- See [SchemaVersion Migration Guide](./schemaversion/README.md) for detailed instructions on creating new schema migrations\n    49→\n    50→#### v1 to v2 Conversion:\n    51→- API version conversions between different Kubernetes API versions\n    52→- Transforms JSON dashboards to structured dashboard format\n    53→- v2 schema is the stable, typed schema with proper type definitions\n    54→- Handles modern dashboard features and Kubernetes-native storage\n    55→\n    56→## Conversion Matrix\n    57→\n    58→The system supports conversions between all dashboard API versions:\n    59→\n    60→| From ↓ / To → | v0alpha1 | v1beta1 | v2alpha1 | v2beta1 |\n    61→|---------------|----------|---------|----------|---------|\n    62→| **v0alpha1**  | ✓        | ✓       | ✓        | ✓       |\n    63→| **v1beta1**   | ✓        | ✓       | ✓        | ✓       |\n    64→| **v2alpha1**  | ✓        | ✓       | ✓        | ✓       |\n    65→| **v2beta1**   | ✓        | ✓       | ✓        | ✓       |\n    66→\n    67→Each conversion path is automatically instrumented with metrics and logging.\n    68→\n    69→## API Versions\n    70→\n    71→The supported dashboard API versions are:\n    72→\n    73→- `dashboard.grafana.app/v0alpha1` - Legacy JSON dashboard format\n    74→- `dashboard.grafana.app/v1beta1` - Migrated JSON dashboard format  \n    75→- `dashboard.grafana.app/v2alpha1` - New structured dashboard format\n    76→- `dashboard.grafana.app/v2beta1` - Enhanced structured dashboard format\n    77→\n    78→## Schema Versions\n    79→\n    80→Schema versions (v13-v42) apply only to v0alpha1 and v1beta1 dashboards:\n    81→\n    82→- **Minimum supported version**: v13\n    83→- **Latest version**: v42  \n    84→- **Migration path**: Sequential (v13→v14→v15...→v42)\n    85→\n    86→For detailed information about creating schema version migrations, see the [SchemaVersion Migration Guide](./schemaversion/README.md).\n    87→\n    88→## Testing\n    89→\n    90→The implementation includes comprehensive test coverage for conversion-level operations:\n    91→\n    92→- **Backend conversion tests**: API version conversions with metrics validation\n    93→- **Frontend tests**: TypeScript conversion tests  \n    94→- **Integration tests**: End-to-end conversion validation\n    95→- **Metrics tests**: Prometheus counter validation\n    96→\n    97→### Backend conversion tests\n    98→\n    99→The backend conversion tests validate API version conversions and metrics instrumentation:\n   100→\n   101→- **API conversion tests**: Test conversions between v0alpha1, v1beta1, v2alpha1, v2beta1\n   102→- **Metrics validation**: Tests verify that conversion metrics are properly recorded\n   103→- **Error handling**: Tests validate error classification and logging\n   104→- **Performance**: Tests ensure conversion operations are efficient\n   105→\n   106→**Test execution:**\n   107→```bash\n   108→# All backend conversion tests\n   109→go test ./apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/conversion/... -v\n   110→\n   111→# Metrics validation tests\n   112→go test ./apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/... -run TestSchemaMigrationMetrics\n   113→```\n   114→\n   115→### Frontend migration comparison tests\n   116→\n   117→The frontend migration comparison tests validate that backend and frontend conversion logic produce consistent results:\n   118→\n   119→- **Test methodology**: Compares backend vs frontend conversion outputs through DashboardModel integration\n   120→- **Dataset coverage**: Tests run against curated test files covering various conversion scenarios\n   121→- **Test location**: `public/app/features/dashboard/state/DashboardMigratorToBackend.test.ts`\n   122→- **Test data**: Located in `apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/testdata/input/` and `testdata/output/`\n   123→\n   124→**Test execution:**\n   125→```bash\n   126→# Frontend migration comparison tests\n   127→yarn test DashboardMigratorToBackend.test.ts\n   128→```\n   129→\n   130→**Test approach:**\n   131→- **Frontend path**: `jsonInput → DashboardModel → DashboardMigrator → getSaveModelClone()`\n   132→- **Backend path**: `jsonInput → Backend Conversion → backendOutput → DashboardModel → getSaveModelClone()`\n   133→- **Comparison**: Direct comparison of final converted states from both paths\n   134→\n   135→For schema version migration testing details, see the [SchemaVersion Migration Guide](./schemaversion/README.md).\n   136→\n   137→## Monitoring Migrations\n   138→\n   139→The dashboard migration system provides comprehensive observability through metrics, logging, and error classification to monitor conversion operations.\n   140→\n   141→### Metrics\n   142→\n   143→The dashboard migration system now provides comprehensive observability through:\n   144→- **Prometheus metrics** for tracking conversion success/failure rates and performance\n   145→- **Structured logging** for debugging and monitoring conversion operations\n   146→- **Automatic instrumentation** via wrapper functions that eliminate code duplication\n   147→- **Error classification** to distinguish between different types of migration failures\n   148→\n   149→#### 1. Dashboard conversion success metric\n   150→\n   151→**Metric Name:** `grafana_dashboard_migration_conversion_success_total`\n   152→\n   153→**Type:** Counter\n   154→\n   155→**Description:** Total number of successful dashboard conversions\n   156→\n   157→**Labels:**\n   158→- `source_version_api` - Source API version (e.g., \"dashboard.grafana.app/v0alpha1\")\n   159→- `target_version_api` - Target API version (e.g., \"dashboard.grafana.app/v1beta1\")\n   160→- `source_schema_version` - Source schema version (e.g., \"16\") - only for v0/v1 dashboards\n   161→- `target_schema_version` - Target schema version (e.g., \"41\") - only for v0/v1 dashboards\n   162→\n   163→**Example:**\n   164→```prometheus\n   165→grafana_dashboard_migration_conversion_success_total{\n   166→  source_version_api=\"dashboard.grafana.app/v0alpha1\",\n   167→  target_version_api=\"dashboard.grafana.app/v1beta1\",\n   168→  source_schema_version=\"16\",\n   169→  target_schema_version=\"41\"\n   170→} 1250\n   171→```\n   172→\n   173→#### 2. Dashboard conversion failure metric\n   174→\n   175→**Metric Name:** `grafana_dashboard_migration_conversion_failure_total`\n   176→\n   177→**Type:** Counter\n   178→\n   179→**Description:** Total number of failed dashboard conversions\n   180→\n   181→**Labels:**\n   182→- `source_version_api` - Source API version\n   183→- `target_version_api` - Target API version  \n   184→- `source_schema_version` - Source schema version (only for v0/v1 dashboards)\n   185→- `target_schema_version` - Target schema version (only for v0/v1 dashboards)\n   186→- `error_type` - Classification of the error (see Error Types section)\n   187→\n   188→**Example:**\n   189→```prometheus\n   190→grafana_dashboard_migration_conversion_failure_total{\n   191→  source_version_api=\"dashboard.grafana.app/v0alpha1\",\n   192→  target_version_api=\"dashboard.grafana.app/v1beta1\",\n   193→  source_schema_version=\"14\",\n   194→  target_schema_version=\"41\",\n   195→  error_type=\"schema_version_migration_error\"\n   196→} 42\n   197→```\n   198→\n   199→### Error Types\n   200→\n   201→The `error_type` label classifies failures into three categories:\n   202→\n   203→#### 1. `conversion_error`\n   204→- General conversion failures not related to schema migration\n   205→- API-level conversion issues\n   206→- Programming errors in conversion functions\n   207→\n   208→#### 2. `schema_version_migration_error`\n   209→- Failures during individual schema version migrations (v14→v15, v15→v16, etc.)\n   210→- Schema-specific transformation errors\n   211→- Data format incompatibilities\n   212→\n   213→#### 3. `schema_minimum_version_error`\n   214→- Dashboards with schema versions below the minimum supported version (< v13)\n   215→- These are logged as warnings rather than errors\n   216→- Indicates dashboards that cannot be migrated automatically\n   217→\n   218→### Logging\n   219→\n   220→#### Log structure\n   221→\n   222→All migration logs use structured logging with consistent field names:\n   223→\n   224→**Base Fields (always present):**\n   225→- `sourceVersionAPI` - Source API version\n   226→- `targetVersionAPI` - Target API version\n   227→- `dashboardUID` - Unique identifier of the dashboard being converted\n   228→\n   229→**Schema Version Fields (v0/v1 dashboards only):**\n   230→- `sourceSchemaVersion` - Source schema version number\n   231→- `targetSchemaVersion` - Target schema version number\n   232→- `erroredSchemaVersionFunc` - Name of the schema migration function that failed (on error)\n   233→\n   234→**Error Fields (failures only):**\n   235→- `errorType` - Same classification as metrics error_type label\n   236→- `erroredConversionFunc` - Name of the conversion function that failed\n   237→- `error` - The actual error message\n   238→\n   239→#### Log levels\n   240→\n   241→##### Success (DEBUG level)\n   242→```json\n   243→{\n   244→  \"level\": \"debug\",\n   245→  \"msg\": \"Dashboard conversion succeeded\",\n   246→  \"sourceVersionAPI\": \"dashboard.grafana.app/v0alpha1\",\n   247→  \"targetVersionAPI\": \"dashboard.grafana.app/v1beta1\",\n   248→  \"dashboardUID\": \"abc123\",\n   249→  \"sourceSchemaVersion\": 16,\n   250→  \"targetSchemaVersion\": 41\n   251→}\n   252→```\n   253→\n   254→##### Conversion/Migration Error (ERROR level)\n   255→```json\n   256→{\n   257→  \"level\": \"error\", \n   258→  \"msg\": \"Dashboard conversion failed\",\n   259→  \"sourceVersionAPI\": \"dashboard.grafana.app/v0alpha1\",\n   260→  \"targetVersionAPI\": \"dashboard.grafana.app/v1beta1\",\n   261→  \"erroredConversionFunc\": \"Convert_V0_to_V1\",\n   262→  \"dashboardUID\": \"abc123\",\n   263→  \"sourceSchemaVersion\": 16,\n   264→  \"targetSchemaVersion\": 41,\n   265→  \"erroredSchemaVersionFunc\": \"V24\",\n   266→  \"errorType\": \"schema_version_migration_error\",\n   267→  \"error\": \"migration failed: table panel plugin not found\"\n   268→}\n   269→```\n   270→\n   271→##### Minimum Version Error (WARN level)\n   272→```json\n   273→{\n   274→  \"level\": \"warn\",\n   275→  \"msg\": \"Dashboard conversion failed\", \n   276→  \"sourceVersionAPI\": \"dashboard.grafana.app/v0alpha1\",\n   277→  \"targetVersionAPI\": \"dashboard.grafana.app/v1beta1\",\n   278→  \"erroredConversionFunc\": \"Convert_V0_to_V1\",\n   279→  \"dashboardUID\": \"def456\",\n   280→  \"sourceSchemaVersion\": 10,\n   281→  \"targetSchemaVersion\": 41,\n   282→  \"erroredSchemaVersionFunc\": \"\",\n   283→  \"errorType\": \"schema_minimum_version_error\",\n   284→  \"error\": \"dashboard schema version 10 cannot be migrated\"\n   285→}\n   286→```\n   287→\n   288→### Implementation Details\n   289→\n   290→#### Automatic instrumentation\n   291→\n   292→All dashboard conversions are automatically instrumented via the `withConversionMetrics` wrapper function:\n   293→\n   294→```go\n   295→// All conversion functions are wrapped automatically\n   296→s.AddConversionFunc((*dashv0.Dashboard)(nil), (*dashv1.Dashboard)(nil),\n   297→    withConversionMetrics(dashv0.APIVERSION, dashv1.APIVERSION, func(a, b interface{}, scope conversion.Scope) error {\n   298→        return Convert_V0_to_V1(a.(*dashv0.Dashboard), b.(*dashv1.Dashboard), scope)\n   299→    }))\n   300→```\n   301→\n   302→#### Error handling\n   303→\n   304→Custom error types provide structured error information:\n   305→\n   306→```go\n   307→// Schema migration errors\n   308→type MigrationError struct {\n   309→    msg            string\n   310→    targetVersion  int\n   311→    currentVersion int\n   312→    functionName   string\n   313→}\n   314→\n   315→// API conversion errors  \n   316→type ConversionError struct {\n   317→    msg               string\n   318→    functionName      string\n   319→    currentAPIVersion string\n   320→    targetAPIVersion  string\n   321→}\n   322→```\n   323→\n   324→### Registration\n   325→\n   326→#### Metrics registration\n   327→\n   328→Metrics must be registered with Prometheus during service initialization:\n   329→\n   330→```go\n   331→import \"github.com/grafana/grafana/apps/dashboard/pkg/migration\"\n   332→\n   333→// Register metrics with Prometheus\n   334→migration.RegisterMetrics(prometheusRegistry)\n   335→```\n   336→\n   337→#### Available metrics\n   338→\n   339→The following metrics are available after Each migration transforms dashboards from one schema version to the next, allowing older dashboards to work with newer Grafana versions.\n    39→\n    40→## Current Schema Versions\n    41→\n    42→- **Minimum Version**: 13\n    43→- **Latest Version**: 42\n    44→- **Backend Location**: `apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/schemaversion/`\n    45→- **Frontend Location**: `v{N}.go` where `{N}` is the target schema version.\n    52→\n    53→```go\n    54→package schemaversion\n    55→\n    56→import \"context\"\n    57→\n    58→// V{N} migrates [describe what this special handling or edge cases\n    64→//\n    65→// Example before migration:\n    66→//\n    67→//\t\"panels\": [\n    68→//\t  {\n    69→//\t    \"id\": 1,\n    70→//\t    \"type\": \"graph\",\n    71→//\t    \"oldProperty\": \"value\"\n    72→//\t  }\n    73→//\t]\n    74→//\n    75→// Example after migration:\n    76→//\n    77→//\t\"panels\": [\n    78→//\t  {\n    79→//\t    \"id\": 1,\n    80→//\t    \"type\": \"graph\",\n    81→//\t    \"newProperty\": \"value\"\n    82→//\t  }\n    83→//\t]\n    84→func V{N}(_ context.Context, dashboard map[string]interface{}) error {\n    85→\tdashboard[\"schemaVersion\"] = {N}\n    86→\n    87→\t// Your migration logic here\n    88→\t// Transform dashboard properties as needed\n    89→\n    90→\treturn nil\n    91→}\n    92→```\n 152→- Panels with different types (graph, table, stat, etc.)\n   153→- Panels with nested panels (rows)\n   154→- Panels with various property combinations\n   155→- Panels with missing or null properties\n   156→- Panels with deprecated properties\n   157→\n   158→**Dashboard-Level Changes:**\n   159→- Dashboard properties (title, tags, time, etc.)\n   160→- Templating variables (query, Transformations:**\n   173→- Property renaming scenarios\n   174→- Property type conversions\n   175→- Property value transformations\n   176→- Property removal scenarios\n   177→- Property addition scenarios\n   178→\n   179→#### Validation Process\n   180→\n   181→1. **Create comprehensive test data** covering all scenarios\n   182→2. **Run backend migration** and capture output\n   183→3. **Run frontend migration** and capture output\n   184→4. **Compare outputs** using strict equality checks\n   185→5. **Iterate on migration logic** until outputs match exactly\n   186→6. **Add more Type Assertions\n   369→```go\n   370→// ❌ Wrong - can panic\n   371→value := panel[\"property\"].(string)\n   372→\n   373→// ✅ Correct - safe type assertion\n   374→if value, ok := panel[\"property\"].(string); ok {\n   375→\t// Use value\n   376→}\n   377→```\n   378→\n   379→### 2. Null Handling\n   380→```go\n   381→// ❌ Wrong - removes intentional nulls\n   382→if value == nil {\n   383→\tdelete(panel, \"property\")\n   384→}\n   385→\n   386→// ✅ Correct - preserve intentional nulls\n   387→if value == nil && !isIntentionallyNull(panel, \"property\") {\n   388→\tdelete(panel, \"property\")\n   389→}\n   390→```\n   391→\n   392→### 3. Nested Panel Processing\n   393→```go\n   394→// ❌ Wrong - misses nested panels\n   395→for _, panel := range panels {\n   396→\tmigratePanel(panel)\n   397→}\n   398→\n   399→// ✅ Correct - handles nested panels\n   400→for _, panel := range panels {\n   401→\tmigratePanelRecursively(panel)\n   402→}\n   403→```\n   404→\n   405→## Migration Beyond v42\n   406→\n   407→**Important**: Schema version 42 is the final version for the v1 dashboard API. For new migrations beyond v42:\n   408→\n   409→1. **Backend**: Continue adding schema versions in Go\n   410→2. **Frontend**: Use panel-specific migration handlers instead of schema versions\n   411→3. **Justification Required**: Each new migration must be properly justified\n   412→4. **v2 Transition**: Consider the impact on the transition to schema v2\n   413→\n   414→### When to Add Schema Migrations vs Panel Migrations\n   415→\n   416→**Add Schema Migration** when:\n   417→- Changes affect dashboard-level properties\n   418→- Cross-panel transformations are needed\n   419→- Backward compatibility requires schema version increment\n   420→- **With proper justification** about why it can't be handled as panel migration\n   421→\n   422→**Use Panel-Specific Migrations** when:\n   423→- Changes are panel-type specific\n   424→- Can be handled within individual panel plugins\n   425→- Don't require dashboard-level schema changes\n   426→\n   427→## Testing Guidelines\n   428→\n   429→### Test File Naming\n   430→- Input: `v{N}.{descriptive_name}.json`\n   431→- Output: `v{N}.{descriptive_name}.v42.json`\n   432→\n   433→### Test Data Requirements\n   434→- Input getSaveModelClone()`\n   492→- **Comparison**: Direct comparison of final migrated states from both paths\n   493→\n   494→### Single Version Migration Testing\n   495→\n   496→The system supports
