The frontend equivalent (`DashboardMigrator.ts`) processes overrides independently of whether `defaults.custom` exists, so dashboards that lack an explicit `defaults.custom` object have their overrides correctly migrated on the frontend but silently skipped on the backend.\n\n## Root Cause\n\n**File:** `apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/schemaversion/v38.go`\n**Function:** `processPanelsV38` (lines 90-137)\n\nThe function chains three sequential type assertions before reaching the override migration call:\n\n```go\n// Line 110-113: gate 1 — fieldConfig must exist\nfieldConfig, ok := p[\"fieldConfig\"].(map[string]interface{})\nif !ok {\n    continue\n}\n\n// Line 115-118: gate 2 — defaults must exist\ndefaults, ok := fieldConfig[\"defaults\"].(map[string]interface{})\nif !ok {\n    continue\n}\n\n// Line 120-123: gate 3 — custom must exist  *** THIS IS THE BUG ***\ncustom, ok := defaults[\"custom\"].(map[string]interface{})\nif !ok {\n    continue   // <-- skips the ENTIRE rest of the loop body, including migrateOverrides()\n}\n\n// Line 125-132: migrate displayMode in defaults.custom (only relevant if custom exists)\nif displayMode, exists := custom[\"displayMode\"]; exists { ... }\n\n// Line 134-135: migrate overrides (SHOULD run even without defaults.custom)\nmigrateOverrides(fieldConfig)\n```\n\nThe `continue` on line 122 aborts the entire panel processing iteration. The `migrateOverrides(fieldConfig)` call on line 135 is unreachable when `defaults[\"custom\"]` is nil or absent, even though overrides live in `fieldConfig[\"overrides\"]` — a completely independent path from `fieldConfig.defaults.custom`.\n\n**Contrast with the frontend** (`public/app/features/dashboard/state/DashboardMigrator.ts`, lines 644-669):\n\n```typescript\nif (panel.type === 'table' && panel.fieldConfig !== undefined) {\n    // defaults.custom migration is guarded independently\n    const displayMode = panel.fieldConfig.defaults?.custom?.displayMode;\n    if (displayMode !== undefined) {\n        // ... migrate defaults.custom.displayMode ...\n    }\n\n    // Override migration runs UNCONDITIONALLY (only gated on fieldConfig existing)\n    if (panel.fieldConfig?.overrides) {\n        for (const override of panel.fieldConfig.overrides) { ... }\n    }\n}\n```\n\nThe frontend uses optional chaining (`?.`) to safely access `defaults.custom.displayMode` without short-circuiting the rest of the function. The override migration block is a separate `if` statement that runs regardless of whether `defaults.custom` exists.\n\n## Evidence\n\n### 1. Backend V38 — bail-out chain (v38.go:120-123)\n\n`apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/schemaversion/v38.go:120-123`:\n```go\ncustom, ok := defaults[\"custom\"].(map[string]interface{})\nif !ok {\n    continue\n}\n```\n\nThis `continue` skips `migrateOverrides(fieldConfig)` at line 135.\n\n### 2. Frontend V38 — independent override processing (DashboardMigrator.ts:658-669)\n\n`public/app/features/dashboard/state/DashboardMigrator.ts:658-669`:\n```typescript\n// Update any overrides referencing the cell display mode\nif (panel.fieldConfig?.overrides) {\n    for (const override of panel.fieldConfig.overrides) {\n        for (let j = 0; j < (override.properties?.length || 0); j++) {\n            let overrideDisplayMode = override.properties[j].value;\n            if (override.properties[j].id === 'custom.displayMode') {\n                override.properties[j].id = 'custom.cellOptions';\n                override.properties[j].value = migrateTableDisplayModeToCellOptions(overrideDisplayMode);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\nThis block is NOT nested inside any `defaults.custom` check.\n\n### 3. Backend test suite only tests panels WITH defaults.custom (v38_test.go)\n\n`apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/schemaversion/v38_test.go`: Every test panel includes an explicit `\"custom\": map[string]interface{}{...}` in defaults. There is no test case for a table panel that has `fieldConfig.overrides` with `custom.displayMode` properties but lacks `fieldConfig.defaults.custom`. This gap allowed the regression to pass tests.\n\n### 4. Post-migration cleanup compounds the issue (frontend_defaults.go:557-662)\n\n`apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/frontend_defaults.go:557-662`: The `filterDefaultValues` function and `cleanupPanelForSaveWithContext` (line 440) interact with a tracking marker `_originallyHadFieldConfigCustom`. When a panel lacks `defaults.custom`:\n\n- `trackPanelOriginalFieldConfigCustom` (line 1151-1158) does NOT set the marker\n- During cleanup, `filterDefaultValues` compares the panel's `fieldConfig` against the default `{\"defaults\": {}, \"overrides\": []}` (line 576-579)\n- If overrides were not migrated (still contain `custom.displayMode` instead of `custom.cellOptions`), the overrides remain as-is\n- However, the un-migrated `custom.displayMode` override property IDs are stale and no longer recognized by the table panel renderer in v10.4, causing the overrides to be silently ignored at render time\n\n### 5. Schema version trigger\n\n`apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/schemaversion/migrations.go:74`: The V38 migration is registered for schema version 38. Dashboards with `schemaVersion` <= 37 trigger this migration during import. The handoff version is 36 (`pkg/kinds/dashboard/addenda.go`), meaning dashboards at version 36 or 37 go through the backend V38 migration path.\n\n## Affected Components\n\n- **`apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/schemaversion/v38.go`** — `processPanelsV38()` function: the bail-out `continue` on line 122 is the root cause\n- **`apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/schemaversion/v38_test.go`** — missing test coverage for panels without `defaults.custom` but with overrides\n- **`apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/migrate.go`** — orchestrates the migration pipeline; calls V38 at line 120-126\n- **`apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/frontend_defaults.go`** — `trackOriginalFieldConfigCustom()` (line 1140) and `filterDefaultValues()` (line 557) interact with the marker system; while not the direct cause, the cleanup logic's reconstruction at line 637-643 creates an empty overrides array `[]interface{}{}` which would also discard overrides if fieldConfig were removed and reconstructed\n- **`public/app/features/dashboard/state/DashboardMigrator.ts`** — the frontend counterpart (lines 644-674) handles this correctly; the discrepancy is backend-only\n\n## Recommendation\n\nThe fix should restructure `processPanelsV38()` in `v38.go` to decouple the `defaults.custom` check from the override migration. The `migrateOverrides(fieldConfig)` call must execute for any table panel that has a `fieldConfig`, regardless of whether `defaults.custom` exists.\n\nSpecifically:\n\n1. **Move `migrateOverrides(fieldConfig)` outside the `defaults[\"custom\"]` guard.** The override migration should be gated only on `fieldConfig` existing (matching the frontend behavior), not on `defaults.custom` existing.\n\n2. **Add a test case** to `v38_test.go` for a table panel with:\n   - `fieldConfig.defaults` set to `{}` (no `custom` key)\n   - `fieldConfig.overrides` containing `custom.displayMode` properties\n   - Expected output: overrides migrated to `custom.cellOptions` format, defaults unchanged\n\n3. The restructured logic should look like:\n   ```go\n   // Migrate defaults.custom.displayMode if custom exists\n   if custom, ok := defaults[\"custom\"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n       if displayMode, exists := custom[\"displayMode\"]; exists {\n           if displayModeStr, ok := displayMode.(string); ok {\n               custom[\"cellOptions\"] = migrateTableDisplayModeToCellOptions(displayModeStr)\n           }\n           delete(custom, \"displayMode\")\n       }\n   }\n   // Always migrate overrides (independent of defaults.custom)\n   migrateOverrides(fieldConfig)\n   ```\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":103,"cache_read_input_tokens":48530,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":103,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ee496dd1-20f7-44bc-9fd1-b5f633d6c91c","uuid":"254f7268-928c-4377-957a-e05a3796dc5a"}

The `migrateOverrides(fieldConfig)` call on line 135 is unreachable when `defaults[\"custom\"]` is nil or absent, even though overrides live in `fieldConfig[\"overrides\"]` — a completely independent path from `fieldConfig.defaults.custom`.\n\n**Contrast with the frontend** (`public/app/features/dashboard/state/DashboardMigrator.ts`, lines 644-669):\n\n```typescript\nif (panel.type === 'table' && panel.fieldConfig !== undefined) {\n    // defaults.custom migration is guarded independently\n    const displayMode = panel.fieldConfig.defaults?.custom?.displayMode;\n    if (displayMode !== undefined) {\n        // ... migrate defaults.custom.displayMode ...\n    }\n\n    // Override migration runs UNCONDITIONALLY (only gated on fieldConfig existing)\n    if (panel.fieldConfig?.overrides) {\n        for (const override of panel.fieldConfig.overrides) { ... }\n    }\n}\n```\n\nThe frontend uses optional chaining (`?.`) to safely access `defaults.custom.displayMode` without short-circuiting the rest of the function. The override migration block is a separate `if` statement that runs regardless of whether `defaults.custom` exists.\n\n## Evidence\n\n### 1. Backend V38 — bail-out chain (v38.go:120-123)\n\n`apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/schemaversion/v38.go:120-123`:\n```go\ncustom, ok := defaults[\"custom\"].(map[string]interface{})\nif !ok {\n    continue\n}\n```\n\nThis `continue` skips `migrateOverrides(fieldConfig)` at line 135.\n\n### 2. Frontend V38 — independent override processing (DashboardMigrator.ts:658-669)\n\n`public/app/features/dashboard/state/DashboardMigrator.ts:658-669`:\n```typescript\n// Update any overrides referencing the cell display mode\nif (panel.fieldConfig?.overrides) {\n    for (const override of panel.fieldConfig.overrides) {\n        for (let j = 0; j < (override.properties?.length || 0); j++) {\n            let overrideDisplayMode = override.properties[j].value;\n            if (override.properties[j].id === 'custom.displayMode') {\n                override.properties[j].id = 'custom.cellOptions';\n                override.properties[j].value = migrateTableDisplayModeToCellOptions(overrideDisplayMode);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n```\n\nThis block is NOT nested inside any `defaults.custom` check.\n\n### 3. Backend test suite only tests panels WITH defaults.custom (v38_test.go)\n\n`apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/schemaversion/v38_test.go`: Every test panel includes an explicit `\"custom\": map[string]interface{}{...}` in defaults. There is no test case for a table panel that has `fieldConfig.overrides` with `custom.displayMode` properties but lacks `fieldConfig.defaults.custom`. This gap allowed the regression to pass tests.\n\n### 4. Post-migration cleanup compounds the issue (frontend_defaults.go:557-662)\n\n`apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/frontend_defaults.go:557-662`: The `filterDefaultValues` function and `cleanupPanelForSaveWithContext` (line 440) interact with a tracking marker `_originallyHadFieldConfigCustom`. When a panel lacks `defaults.custom`:\n\n- `trackPanelOriginalFieldConfigCustom` (line 1151-1158) does NOT set the marker\n- During cleanup, `filterDefaultValues` compares the panel's `fieldConfig` against the default `{\"defaults\": {}, \"overrides\": []}` (line 576-579)\n- If overrides were not migrated (still contain `custom.displayMode` instead of `custom.cellOptions`), the overrides remain as-is\n- However, the un-migrated `custom.displayMode` override property IDs are stale and no longer recognized by the table panel renderer in v10.4, causing the overrides to be silently ignored at render time\n\n### 5. Schema version trigger\n\n`apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/schemaversion/migrations.go:74`: The V38 migration is registered for schema version 38. Dashboards with `schemaVersion` <= 37 trigger this migration during import. The handoff version is 36 (`pkg/kinds/dashboard/addenda.go`), meaning dashboards at version 36 or 37 go through the backend V38 migration path.\n\n## Affected Components\n\n- **`apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/schemaversion/v38.go`** — `processPanelsV38()` function: the bail-out `continue` on line 122 is the root cause\n- **`apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/schemaversion/v38_test.go`** — missing test coverage for panels without `defaults.custom` but with overrides\n- **`apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/migrate.go`** — orchestrates the migration pipeline; calls V38 at line 120-126\n- **`apps/dashboard/pkg/migration/frontend_defaults.go`** — `trackOriginalFieldConfigCustom()` (line 1140) and `filterDefaultValues()` (line 557) interact with the marker system; while not the direct cause, the cleanup logic's reconstruction at line 637-643 creates an empty overrides array `[]interface{}{}` which would also discard overrides if fieldConfig were removed and reconstructed\n- **`public/app/features/dashboard/state/DashboardMigrator.ts`** — the frontend counterpart (lines 644-674) handles this correctly; the discrepancy is backend-only\n\n## Recommendation\n\nThe fix should restructure `processPanelsV38()` in `v38.go` to decouple the `defaults.custom` check from the override migration. The `migrateOverrides(fieldConfig)` call must execute for any table panel that has a `fieldConfig`, regardless of whether `defaults.custom` exists.\n\nSpecifically:\n\n1. **Move `migrateOverrides(fieldConfig)` outside the `defaults[\"custom\"]` guard.** The override migration should be gated only on `fieldConfig` existing (matching the frontend behavior), not on `defaults.custom` existing.\n\n2. **Add a test case** to `v38_test.go` for a table panel with:\n   - `fieldConfig.defaults` set to `{}` (no `custom` key)\n   - `fieldConfig.overrides` containing `custom.displayMode` properties\n   - Expected output: overrides migrated to `custom.cellOptions` format, defaults unchanged\n\n3. The restructured logic should look like:\n   ```go\n   // Migrate defaults.custom.displayMode if custom exists\n   if custom, ok := defaults[\"custom\"].(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n       if displayMode, exists := custom[\"displayMode\"]; exists {\n           if displayModeStr, ok := displayMode.(string); ok {\n               custom[\"cellOptions\"] = migrateTableDisplayModeToCellOptions(displayModeStr)\n           }\n           delete(custom, \"displayMode\")\n       }\n   }\n   // Always migrate overrides (independent of defaults.custom)\n   migrateOverrides(fieldConfig)\n   ```\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
